# Fiesta 2016 server reconstruction: overview, index and bug register (X01)

Entry point of the behavioural specification reconstructed from the 2016 server binaries (`Files/Server/2016`). The
per-subsystem documents (`login-server.md`, `login/`, `wm/`, `zone/`, `db/`, `shared/`) hold the pseudocode; this file
ties them together: which process does what, which messages a feature crosses, the vocabulary, the statements later
items corrected, and every behaviour of the 2016 servers that looks unintended.

Evidence levels as in `AGENTS.md`: plain = read in the 2016 code (2016 server PDB names count as code),
*(captured)*, *(pdb2017)*, *(fh-doc)*, *(db-backup)*, *(inferred)*.

> Status: X01 is written in parts (`docs/plan/status/X01.md`). Sections marked *(part n)* are not written yet.

## 1. Reading guide

*(part 4)* Map of the documents per server process and subsystem.

## 2. Architecture overview

*(part 4)* Processes, links between them, data flow, persistence.

## 3. Cross-server sequence index

*(parts 5–6)* Feature → messages → handler in each server.

## 4. Glossary

*(part 7)*

## 5. Superseded statements

Finished items are not edited. When a later item finds an earlier statement wrong, it records the correction in its
knowledge file (sections named "Corrections", in `docs/knowledge/*.md`), in its own pseudocode, or in P02's capture
tables. This section collects all of them. Read it before you trust an older file at the place listed. Columns: *Older
place* = file and § holding the old text; *Superseded statement* = what that file says (shortened); *Current
statement* = what the 2016 code (or a capture) shows; *Source* = the item and § with the evidence. A row marked
"(addition)" fills a gap the older file left open and does not contradict it. Statements that were only renamed
(S01's official names) are in §4 and `docs/knowledge/S01-renames.tsv`, not here. Corrections that change a bug
entry are also marked in the register (§6.1) and listed in §5.9.

Sources collected: knowledge `L01`, `W01`, `W03`, `W05`, `Z02`, `Z07`, `Z08`, `Z09`, `Z10`, `Z12`, `Z13`, `Z15`, `Z16`,
`Z17`, `Z18`, `Z21`, `Z22`, `Z24`, `Z25`, `Z29`, `D02`, `D06`, `S01` §3.3, `S02`, `C01` §3, `P02` (with
`docs/protocol/capture-validation.md` §7 / §9), and the correcting passages in `W05-process.md` §5.1 / §5.3,
`Z29-exp-tables.md` §2.6, `Z12-stats.md` §1.3, `Z25-skill-specials.md`, `Z09-world.md`, `D06-db-inferred.md`. X01 adds
one: W01-B5 (§5.3).

### 5.1 Protocol README and tools (P01)

| Older place | Superseded statement | Current statement | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `docs/protocol/README.md` §1 seed table, §2 sequence, "Unknowns" | the zone sends `0x0807` only as the reply to client `0x0806`; no call from the accept path | the zone sends `0x0807` on accept: `ZoneServer::zs_connect` FUN_005aa840 → `ClientSession::Send_NC_MISC_SEED_ACK` FUN_0045b120, value from `ShinePlayer::so_EncSeedSet` FUN_0054f660 (global counter, +1, 0 at 499). The client never sends `0x0806`; that handler uses another counter and is reachable only in a broken session state (Z18-B2) *(captured)* | P02; Z18 §1.3; capture-validation §2.3 |
| `README.md` §1 heartbeat | the client sends `0x0804`, the server answers `0x0805` | the server (WM, zone) sends `0x0804` after 30 s of client silence (`ClientSession::CheckHeartBeat` FUN_0045b110 on the zone); the client answers `0x0805`. The client also sends unsolicited `0x0805` to the WM while loading a map *(captured)* | P02; capture-validation §2.4 |
| `README.md` §2 flow | (gaps) | missing from the sketch: `0x0C04` / `0x0C05` after the login request, the token-login order, the 10-s `0x0C1B` world-status poll, `0x080D` / `0x080E` game time, the WM option pushes `0x7032`–`0x7034`, the 15 blob copies before `0x1802`, map change, logout to the world list *(captured)* (addition) | capture-validation §7 |
| `layouts.json` (zone, generated) | s2c `0x0E0D` sent from `smh_SendFurnitureInfo` | false positive: a local byte array `{3, 0xd, 0xe, 0x10}`, no packet | Z09 §28.9 |
| `handlers.json` / catalog | 0x1002 = `NC_CHAR_LOGIN_ACK` (REQ+1 guess) | 0x1002 = `NC_CHAR_LOGINFAIL_ACK {u16 error}`, 0x1003 = `NC_CHAR_LOGIN_ACK {char[16] zoneIp, u16 zonePort}` (client FUN_004769d0 / FUN_00476190; W01 §4.2 agrees) | C01 knowledge §3 |
| `handlers.json` (character) | 0xB411 "…ITEM_USE_ACK" | 0xB411 is the DB request `NC_USER_CONNECTION_DB_CHANGE_CHAR_ID_ITEM_USE_REQ` by its PDB struct; 0xB40A = `NC_USER_CONNECTION_DB_CHANGE_CHAR_ID_REQ` | D02 knowledge |
| `pcap_decode.py` | annotations matched only for `C→S` / `S→C`; struct-array types counted as 1 byte | fixed by P02 (parts 2 and 5): `X→C` / `C→X` labels mapped, struct arrays, `bytes`, nested and inline types decoded | capture-validation §4, §11 |

|---|---|---|---|

| §2 `main` FUN_00402f30 | chdir, LoadServerInfo, HandleServiceCommand, LoadVersionKeys, start the main thread | `LoginServer::Start`: chdir, `ViewStartInfo`, `ReadDefineScriptor` (−1), `ParseCmd` (−2), `ReadGlobalGameData` (−4), `WinService::startDispatcher`; the main thread is started by `::OnStart_Service` | L01 §1.8, S02 §4.3 |
| §3.1 | `lastRecvTime` set per dispatched packet | set on every read completion: a peer trickling a partial frame keeps its heartbeat alive | S02 §1.6 |
| §3.2 last line | a failing `<Peer>_CheckAlive` → "the packet is ignored" | the handler returns 0 and the parser closes the link / client (`m_Status = 3`) | L01 §4.1 |
| §3.2 | unregistered opcodes on server links are ignored | ignored for AccountDB / AccountLogDB / OPTool; **WM link closed** (`fc_dummyfunction` FUN_004080d0 returns 0) | L01 §4.1 |
| §4 0x0C06 / 0x0C18 | no-op | both return 0: the client is closed | L01 §4.4 |
| §4 0x0C37 | `if len != 0x22: return` | returns 0 (after a failed `CheckConnectionValidation`): the client is closed | L01 knowledge |
| §4 / §5.3 `g_TokenTable` | `ExpireOlderThan`, `Find`, `NoteFailure(ip)`, `Insert`, `Remove`, `ContainsUser` | `COTPLogin::DelByDueTime`, `Find`, **`DelByIP`** (deletes every pending token of that IP, L01-B9), `Add`, `DelByOTP`, `IsAddable` | L01 §4.3 |
| §5.1 0x0801 | reply 0x0802 `{myServerId, myWorld, myZone}` | `{echo_data 0, server_to_id (0 AccountDB / 1 AccountLog), server_from_id 4, world, zone, u16 key = to + from}` | L01 §4.2; W01 knowledge |
| §5.3 FUN_0040a8a0 | token = `sprintf("%d%d%d%d%d%c", random…)` | token = MD5 hex (32 lower-case digits) of `sprintf("%d%d%d%d%d%c", ip, m_IncreaseSeed, userNo, dueTime, rand(), 0)`; `MakeOTP` never fails, so 0x1E59 is unreachable | L01 §4.3 |
| §5.3 0x0C36 | `{u16 wmHandle, u16 result, char token[32]}` | `{u16 result, u16 wmHandle, char token[32]}` (result first; Login FUN_00408a20 and WM FUN_00424a70 agree) | W01 knowledge |
| §5.4 0x2812 / 0x2814 | "further status queries" | `NC_OPTOOL_LOGIN_USER_RATABLE_GET_REQ` / `_SET_CMD`: the world-load thresholds `m_LoginUserRatable` (6 × u16 `LOGIN_USER_RATABLE`; S02 §4.3's "6 ints" is wrong too) | L01 §3, L01 knowledge |
| §5.4 0x2818 | "further status queries" | `NC_OPTOOL_LOGON_PROCESS_TIME_VIEW_ACK`, six u32 counters, all sent as 0 (L01-B10) | L01 §4.4 |
| §8 (client 0x0C67) | the client sends `0x0C04` on `0x0C67` | the client sends its login request (`0x0C5A` / `0x0C37`) first, then `0x0C04` (client FUN_0046f470) *(captured)* | P02; capture-validation §3.1 |
| `annotations/L00.json` 0x0C37 | `u8[32] token` | `char[32]`: the ASCII token of WM `0x0C34`, no NUL (`annotations/P02.json` overrides) *(captured)* | P02; capture-validation §3.2 |
| `packets-login.md` WORLD (L00 annotation) | status "set to 2 at load; world select fails while status < 5" | `InitWorldManagerInfo` FUN_00409eb0 sets **0** (`WMSUS_CLOSED`) for a world with a WM client-port row and 2 (`WMSUS_EMPTY`) only without one (`annotations/L01.json` overrides) | L01 §2.5, §3 |

| W01 core §2 (link loss) | a lost CharDB link is reconnected | `CLOSE_CHAR_DB`: one immediate reconnect, then the WM shuts down; a lost AccountDB link (once linked) shuts it down (close reason 0x789); AccountLogDB / GameLogDB only reconnect | W05 §1.5 |

Consecutive world logins use consecutive slots of the 0x800-slot table; a slot is overwritten only by the 2048th later select of that world. W01-B5 does not exist as stated | L01 §2.5; P02 knowledge |

| W01 §4.1 delete, annotation 0x1408 | 0x1407 sends CharDB 0x1408 `{handle, charNo @2, u8 slot @6}` | 0x1407 (FUN_00412fa0) sends **0x1413** `NC_AVATAR_GUILD_DATA_REQ {u16 clienthandle, u32 chrregnum, u8 slot}`; 0x1408 is sent only after the guild check (0x1414 / 0x1416), 15 bytes `{u16 clienthandle, u32 usrregnum, u32 avatar, u8 slot, u32 nNo}`; guild masters get 0x140A {0x00C7}, unknown guild 0x00C9, member missing 0x00C3 | W03 §4.5, S01 §3.3 |

| W01 close reasons | (addition) | 0x781 unknown client opcode, 0x782 service stop, 0x786 default (set once per slot, W05-B9), 0x787 packet while `m_Status ≠ 1`, 0x789 AccountDB lost, 0x78b CharDB lost; 0x78a / 0x78c unused | W05 §4.5 |

| W03 §8.4 / Z08 §8.2 | tournament refunds paid | no 2016 component pays the refund that 0x74F8 announces (zone 0x74F4 handler empty, DB procedures get no money), unless the lost 2016 `setChoice` did (D06-B3) | D06 |

| W05 §1.4 pump | `session.vtbl[…]` calls | slots of the session's `m_Packet` (`CPacket` table) | S02 knowledge |

### 5.7 DB servers (D01–D05) and the backups

D06 compared every 2016 call with both backup versions; for a rebuild its version choices replace the earlier items'
"Character.bak used" statements (D06 §8).

| Older place | Superseded statement | Current statement | Source |
|---|---|---|---|

| D01 §3.4 / §7 0x0C26 TEENAGER | nType 1 minutes *(inferred)* | `(int)(17940 − elapsed) / 60` confirmed in the asm (0x4029c6); user key ≤ 24 characters (25-byte output buffer) | D06 |
| D01 §4.2 L20 codes; D01-B10 | `nRet` uninitialised; codes guessed | both wrappers preset `nRet = −1`; Get > 0 already rewarded (0x1289), −1 error (0x128a), 0 → Set; Set 0 granted (0x1288), 1 / 3 / other → 0x128b *(inferred)*; the log condition is intended | D06 §3.2, §3.3 |

## 6. Original-server bugs

Every behaviour of the 2016 servers that looks unintended, so a rebuild can decide per entry to reproduce it (faithful
rebuild) or fix it (deliberate change). One row per bug, keeping the owning item's ID `<item>-B<n>`; the owning item's
document holds the full description where the behaviour is tagged `(original bug) <ID>`.

**Sources.** §6.1 is generated by `docs/plan/status/X01-register.py write` from every table row whose first cell is a
`<item>-B<n>` ID in `docs/pseudocode/**/*.md`, `docs/knowledge/*.md` and P02's table in
`docs/protocol/capture-validation.md` §8 (re-run it after items change; do not edit §6.1 by hand). IDs that
capture-validation lists as "shown on the wire" get *(captured)* with the § of the capture. §6.2 is hand-written:
bugs X01 found in free-text notes, client-side mismatches and client text typos. The row of the owning item's own file wins; other files that repeat the ID are named as "also"
(Z19 repeats S02-B11…B21, S01 §1.4 repeats W04-B2/B3/B22/B29 and S01-B1…B3 and is skipped). The verdict tables in
`knowledge/Z09.md` and `knowledge/Z13.md` reuse bug IDs but are not bug rows and are skipped. Entries a later item
changed are marked **Superseded**, **Narrowed** or **Extended** with the change and a link to §5.9.

**Columns.** *Handler*: the owning row's handler cell (opcode, function, `@ FUN_…`, § of the owning document) plus a
link to the owning document's bug section. *Evidence*: `code` = read in the 2016 code; `code; effect *(inferred)*` =
the behaviour is read in the code, its consequence deduced; *(inferred)* = the behaviour itself is deduced;
*(db-backup)* = the DB side comes from the private-server backups, not the 2016 build. None of these rows was observed
in a capture unless marked *(captured)*. *Owner*: the item whose document describes it.

### 6.2 Entries from free-text notes and client-side mismatches

Hand-written by X01 (part 2). Sources: the free text of the items written before the `(original bug)` convention
(L00, W01, Z01–Z03, C01, C02, P01, P03; grep for `bug`, `sic`, `never reach`, `uninitialised`, `ignored`,
`copy-paste`, `typo`), `(sic)` notes of later items that no bug row covers, C01 §5 (server → client opcodes no 2016
client dispatcher compares, re-checked against `re-data/client-handlers.json` and the owning documents), and the client
texts of `docs/protocol/client-error-codes.json`. Everything the pre-convention items described in free text already
has a row in their bug tables (§6.1), except the entries below. P02-B1…B4 now sit in §6.1.10 (the generator reads
P02's table in `docs/protocol/capture-validation.md` §8). Error *codes* a server sends that the client shows no text
for are recorded per handler by the owning items (their rows say "no client text") and are not repeated here.

Not client-bound after all (C01 §5 counted them as dropped client packets): 0x0E0D and 0x3214 are no packets (local
byte arrays: `smh_SendFurnitureInfo`, Z09 §28.9; `CPetData::GetPetActionCaseFlexible` FUN_004cf290, a raw 3-byte
store *(inferred)*), 0xA413 goes to the Character server (Z09 §28.12, W04-B43), 0x244C to the WM (Z03 §8.1),
0xA415 to the Character server (W04-B41), 0x083A is never handled by the zone (W04-B56). Part 3 lists this as a
correction of C01 §5.
